On NER, VEXMLM raises OOV-token entity accuracy from 81.4\% to 94.3\%, averaged over 11 of the 19 evaluated languages for which OOV analysis was possible. Our contributions are: (i) a vocabulary-extension and embedding-initialization procedure tailored to Ge'ez script; (ii) a two-stage training strategy under which vocabulary and continued-pretraining gains on Amharic/Tigrinya transfer to 17 typologically related, unaugmented African languages; and (iii) an evaluation spanning both intrinsic tokenization metrics (vocabulary coverage, fertility, OOV rate) and extrinsic task performance across all 19 languages.

</details>


### [148] [In-Place Tokenizer Expansion for Pre-trained LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15232)
*Jimmy T. H. Smith,Tarek Dakhran,Alberto Cabrera,Simon S. Lee,Paul Pak,Aditya Tadimeti,Tim Seyde,Maxime Labonne,Alexander Amini,Mathias Lechner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出tokenizer扩展方法，通过继续BPE合并和两阶段适应，解决小词汇量模型在添加新语言时的token碎片化问题，显著提升Hindi、Vietnamese等语言的解码速度，并验证了扩展流程的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定tokenizer在预训练初期基于语料分配词汇，当语言优先级变化时，新增语言需分割为更多tokens，导致延迟、计算和能耗上升；紧凑模型因embedding和LM-head矩阵参数占比高，被迫采用小词汇量，加剧非目标语言的语言碎片化问题。

Method: 在现有tokenizer基础上，继续在多语言语料上执行BPE合并，确保源token多数保持不变；新token初始化为源子token embeddings的均值；采用两阶段适应：先进行embedding-only微调，再开展全模型持续预训练，以恢复源模型质量。

Result: 应用于LFM2-8B-A1B模型（8B参数混合专家模型），扩展至128K tokenizer；Hindi和Vietnamese的token数量减少约2.4倍和2.6倍（泰语最高达4.0倍）；结合更大词汇量的每token成本，估计在参考设备上实现每字符解码加速2.2至3.7倍。

Conclusion: 成功发布扩展后的模型权重和tokenizer，实证方法可有效提升多语言效率；同时报告负结果以优化流程，为小模型tokenizer升级提供实用方案。

Abstract: A tokenizer fixed at the start of pre-training allocates vocabulary in proportion to the pre-training corpus, reflecting the deployment priorities at that time. When those priorities shift, languages added later are split into many more tokens per word, which can raise latency, compute, and energy consumption for users of those languages. Cloud models can afford a broad vocabulary because the embedding and LM-head matrices are a small fraction of their parameters. On a compact model those matrices are a material share of per-token decode bandwidth, so on-device models ship small vocabularies and accept fragmentation outside a fixed language set. We present tokenizer expansion, an in-place recipe for upgrading a pre-trained model's tokenizer when the model producer controls its design. We continue the existing tokenizer's BPE merges on a multilingual corpus, so most source tokens carry over unchanged as single tokens and every new token has an exact decomposition into source tokens. We copy the carried-over embedding rows unchanged and initialize new rows as the mean of their source sub-token embeddings. A two-stage adaptation, embedding-only training then full-model continued pre-training, recovers source-checkpoint quality. We apply the recipe to a continued pre-trained checkpoint of LFM2-8B-A1B, an 8B-parameter Mixture-of-Experts model, to help produce LFM2.5-8B-A1B with a 128K tokenizer. The expanded tokenizer encodes Hindi and Vietnamese in roughly $2.4\times$ and $2.6\times$ fewer tokens than the source (up to $4.0\times$ on Thai). Combining these reductions with the measured per-token cost of the larger vocabulary, we estimate a $2.2$-$3.7\times$ per-character decode speedup for these languages across our reference devices. We release the model weights and the expanded tokenizer, and report the negative findings that shaped the recipe.

</details>


### [149] [Language Identification via Compositional Data Analysis: A Linear-Time Classifier Based on Log-Ratio Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15238)
*Paul-Andrei Pogăcean,Sanda-Maria Avram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于居中log-ratio变换的组成向量方法，将字符和双字母频率建模为单纯形约束向量，结合Laplace平滑解决稀疏性，在六种语言上实现高效准确的语言识别，尤其适合资源受限场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言识别领域，神经方法计算资源需求大，而经典频率方法虽高效但距离度量（如欧氏距离）对组成数据不适用，需开发更合适的距离度量以兼顾效率与准确性。

Method: 将字符和双字母频率分布建模为组成向量（约束于单纯形），通过居中log-ratio (CLR)变换双射映射到ℝ^D的(D-1)维零和子空间，使欧氏距离等价于Aitchison距离；提出管道结合CLR变换的单字母和双字母特征，并用Laplace平滑缓解稀疏性；在六种语言数据集上验证。

Result: 实验显示，所提方法在不同文本长度下均取得稳健准确率，尤其对长序列表现突出（如长文本中准确率显著提升），验证了其在多样文本长度下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 组成表示提供确定性、计算高效的替代方案，避免了神经网络的高资源消耗，同时保持可解释性，特别适用于强调低资源消耗和透明度的应用环境。

Abstract: Language identification is commonly addressed using either neural architectures or statistical n-gram models. Neural approaches typically require substantial computational resources, whereas classical frequency-based methods offer efficient linear-time performance, but rely on distance metrics that are not always appropriate for compositional data.
  This work models character and bigram frequency distributions as compositional vectors constrained to the simplex and mapped via the centered log-ratio (CLR) transformation bijectively onto the $(D-1)$-dimensional zero-sum subspace of $\mathbb{R}^D$, where Euclidean distances correspond to Aitchison distances. A pipeline is proposed, combining CLR-transformed unigram and bigram features with Laplace smoothing to address sparsity. The method is evaluated on six languages.
  Experimental results show that the proposed approach achieves robust accuracy across different text lengths, with strong performance for longer sequences. These findings indicate that compositional representations provide a deterministic and computationally efficient alternative for language identification, particularly in settings where interpretability and low resource consumption are essential.

</details>


### [150] [TikStance: A Multimodal and Hierarchical Dataset for Multi-target Stance Analysis in TikTok Political Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15240)
*Yazhi Zhang,Fuqiang Niu,Bowen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出TikStance数据集，包含161个TikTok政治视频及13,876条层级评论，覆盖2024年美国选举三位核心人物（特朗普、拜登、哈里斯），支持多模态立场检测研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频平台政治讨论激增，但现有数据集缺乏视听信息与层级对话的联合保留，制约计算分析进展。亟需构建兼顾多模态特征与上下文关联的政治讨论数据集。

Method: 构建TikStance数据集：1）采集2023年9月-2025年1月关于三位政治人物的短视频及评论树；2）建立视频元数据与层级评论关联模型；3）采用三人独立标注机制，使用Favor/Against/None三类标签体系进行视频/评论立场标注，并通过争议样本重标确保一致性。

Result: 数据集覆盖23.3%嵌套回复深度评论，三位政治人物的Krippendorff's α系数分别达0.743/0.723/0.722，显示标注可靠性；发现目标人物相关的立场分布与对话深度显著差异。

Conclusion: TikStance通过多目标覆盖、层级对话建模及可信多层次标注，突破短视频政治分析瓶颈，为多模态立场检测、政治传播学和上下文感知NLP研究提供关键基础设施。

Abstract: Political discourse has increasingly moved to short-video platforms, yet computational analysis of such content remains constrained by the scarcity of datasets that jointly preserve audiovisual information and hierarchical conversations. Here we present TikStance, a multimodal and context-aware dataset comprising 161 videos and 13,876 comments from TikTok, designed for stance detection in political discussions. The dataset covers three major political figures in the 2024 U.S. election cycle--Donald Trump, Joe Biden, and Kamala Harris--with content collected between September 2023 and January 2025. Each discussion unit links a host video and its metadata to a parent-linked comment tree, enabling stance analysis within both audiovisual and conversational context. Each item was independently labeled by three annotators using a three-class scheme (Favor, Against, None) for video-to-target and comment-to-target stance; items with disagreement were re-annotated, and the final Krippendorff's \(α\) reached 0.743, 0.723, and 0.722 for the Trump, Biden, and Harris subsets, respectively. Descriptive analysis further reveals target-dependent differences in stance distributions and conversational depth, with nested replies accounting for 23.3\% of all comments. By combining multi-target coverage, hierarchical conversations, and reliable multi-level human annotations, TikStance supports research in multimodal stance detection, political communication, computational social science, and context-aware natural language processing.

</details>


### [151] [Beyond the Leaderboard: Design Lessons for Trustworthy Multimodal VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15241)
*Sushant Gautam,Vajira Thambawita,Michael A. Riegler,Pål Halvorsen,Steven A. Hicks*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 医疗多模态AI需融合视觉与文本证据以实现可靠可解释性；GI内镜案例研究显示参数高效适应虽挑战性能强，但推理不忠实完整；结构化推理方法更可靠；呼吁改进评估体系、数据治理与鲁棒性检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗多模态AI必须同时整合视觉和文本证据，并保持可靠性与可解释性，以满足临床决策需求，但现有系统在问答和解释质量上存在不足。

Method: 基于MediaEval Medico 2025的回顾性GI内镜检查案例研究，系统分析九个已记录系统在问答和解释质量方面的设计选择，重点考察参数高效适应与结构化推理方法。

Result: 预训练骨干的参数高效适应在挑战中表现强劲，但答案级增益未能一致转化为忠实完整的临床推理；强制结构化推理和显式接地的方法在异质问题类型上展现更高可靠性，但证据仅为相关性而非消融测试验证。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调需超越词汇重叠的评估标准，推行标准化证据链接解释、泄漏感知数据治理及轻量级鲁棒性与校准检查，以推动基于数据融合、可解释性和弹性评估的可信多模态医疗AI发展。

Abstract: Healthcare multimodal AI must combine visual and textual evidence while remaining reliable and interpretable. Using MediaEval Medico 2025 as a retrospective GI endoscopy case study, we analyze design choices across nine documented systems for question answering and explanation quality. Parameter-efficient adaptation of pretrained backbones provides strong challenge performance, but answer-level gains do not consistently translate into faithful and complete clinical reasoning. Methods enforcing structured reasoning and explicit grounding show more reliable behavior across heterogeneous question types, although the evidence is correlational rather than ablation-based. These results motivate evaluation beyond lexical overlap, standardized evidence-linked explanations, leakage-aware data governance, and lightweight robustness and calibration checks. The findings support trustworthy multimodal healthcare AI based on data fusion, explainability, and resilient evaluation.

</details>


### [152] [SciDiagramEdit: Learning to Edit Scientific Diagrams from Paper Revisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15272)
*Yasheng Sun,Zezi Zeng,Yifan Yang,Chong Luo,Wenyi Wang,Ziwei Liu,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SciDiagramEdit框架，通过代理技能进化实现科学图表的自然语言指令自动编辑，利用arXiv修订历史挖掘训练数据，显著提升编辑准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编辑研究论文中的图表是常规但耗时的任务，涉及重标组件、调整布局等；自动化该流程极具挑战，因为科学图表是密集信息载体，包含示意图、图表、照片等异构元素，需遵循严格视觉语法传达论点，而自然语言指令驱动的自动化缺乏有效训练信号。

Method: 提出SciDiagramEdit基准与技能进化框架：1) 从arXiv版本历史中挖掘前后图表对，每个修订对基于作者真实修改意图；2) 采用代理学习方法，通过技能进化机制——代理提议者基于执行轨迹在多轮迭代中持续优化技能规范；3) 操作在图表矢量源上，支持用户检查并协同编辑基础图元。

Result: 在保留验证集上，技能进化使编辑准确率逐步提升，实验证据表明自然论文修订可作为有效训练信号，适用于多样化编辑指令。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了利用自然论文修订进行指令驱动图表编辑的可行性，为自动化科研图表编辑提供了创新途径，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Editing the figures in a research paper is a routine and time-consuming part of everyday research practice: authors relabel components, rearrange panels, and restyle visuals as they revise their manuscripts. Automating this editing workflow under a natural-language instruction, however, is challenging, because a scientific figure is a dense infographic in which heterogeneous visual elements such as schematics, plots, photos, captions, and arrows are composed under a tight visual grammar to advance a specific argument. To address this, we present SciDiagramEdit, a benchmark and skill-evolution framework that learns from natural paper revisions and operates on the figure's editable vector source, where users can inspect and co-edit individual primitives alongside the agent. Our benchmark mines before/after figure pairs from arXiv version histories, each grounded in the authors' own revision intent. To accommodate the diversity of editing instructions, we adopt agentic learning via skill evolution: an agentic proposer continually refines the agent's skill specification from execution traces over multiple epochs. The resulting skill progressively lifts edit accuracy on a held-out validation set, providing evidence that natural paper revisions are an effective training signal for instruction-driven figure editing.

</details>


### [153] [Partition, Prompt, Aggregate: Statistical Self-Consistency in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15277)
*Patrik Wolf,Thomas Kleine Buening,Andreas Krause,Celestine Mendler-Dünner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在上下文学习中违反全概率法则，表现出"宏观谬误"现象：细粒度子群体估计的组合结果比直接总体估计更贴近人类数据，表明模型知识传播存在缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证LLM的上下文学习是否满足概率推理的基本自洽性原则——全概率法则，即各子群体条件分布加权汇总应等于总体边缘分布。

Method: 采用二叉树结构递归划分总体为多级子群体，通过自然语言提示LLM生成子群体估计值，逐级聚合对比不同粒度划分下的总体估计一致性。

Result: 在多个领域和前沿模型中普遍发现违反全概率法则的现象；细粒度子群体估计组合（宏观谬误）显著优于直接总体估计，该效应具有跨任务鲁棒性且可通过隐式提示部分缓解。

Conclusion: 模型虽掌握子群体知识但缺乏向总体层面的有效传播机制，提出统计自洽性作为无需外部标注的新型LLM评估维度。

Abstract: In-context learning is commonly interpreted as a form of conditional inference, in which the prompt specifies a context and the model's output is treated as an estimate of the corresponding conditional distribution. If this interpretation holds, then LLM estimates should satisfy basic probabilistic identities. In particular, the law of total probability asserts that prior-weighted conditional distributions aggregate into population-level marginals over any valid partition of the population. In this work, we investigate to what extent LLM estimates adhere to this self-consistency principle. We use binary trees as an evaluation scaffold to recursively partition a population into increasingly fine-grained subpopulations. We then prompt LLMs with verbalized subpopulation descriptions in context, aggregate the resulting estimates back into population-level estimates, and compare them across partitions of varying granularity. Applying this protocol across problem domains and state-of-the-art frontier models, we show widespread violations of basic consistency properties. An in-depth study of persona prompting reveals a pattern we call the macro fallacy: estimates reconstructed from more fine-grained subpopulation responses are often better aligned with human reference data than direct population-level estimates. This effect persists across variations in tree structure and estimation task, and can be partially recovered through implicit prompting. Together, these findings suggest that models possess relevant subpopulation knowledge but do not reliably propagate it into aggregate estimates. This gap establishes statistical self-consistency as an unsaturated, reference-free criterion for evaluating LLMs.

</details>

### [154] [Tight Wavelet Frames on Graphs via Isometric Group Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14168)
*Rigobert Fokam Souop,Laurent Bitjoka*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: This work introduces a novel graph wavelet construction via embedding into Cayley graphs, enabling exact Fourier analysis on the host. The spectral band-pass wavelets form tight frames, ensure exact reconstruction, and achieve superior localization while eliminating frame inversion requirements, with applications validated through numerical experiments.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional spectral graph wavelets suffer from non-canonical eigenbases, lack of tight frames, and computationally expensive frame inversion for reconstruction. An exact analytical framework was needed to overcome these limitations on irregular graphs.

Method: Embedding the target graph into a Cayley graph of a finite abelian group to utilize classical Fourier analysis; constructing either dilation wavelets (via group automorphisms) or spectral band-pass wavelets (using dual-frequency filter banks); signal completion via discrete harmonic extension minimizing Dirichlet energy.

Result: Proven tight-frame identity and exact reconstruction capability; multiresolution decomposition with O(JN log N) complexity via host FFT; band-pass wavelets localize 89-99% energy within graph-distance 2; machine-precision reconstruction achieved on benchmarks.

Conclusion: The proposed method establishes a robust framework for graph wavelet analysis by leveraging Cayley graph embeddings, achieving theoretical guarantees (tight frames, exact reconstruction) and practical efficiency while overcoming limitations of traditional spectral approaches.

Abstract: Spectral graph wavelets apply a kernel to the graph Laplacian spectrum. On an irregular graph their analyzing functions inherit a non-canonical eigenbasis, they do not form a tight frame, and reconstruction requires inverting a frame operator. We take a different route, built on an exact substrate. Given an isometric embedding of a connected graph into a Cayley graph of a finite abelian group, a host on which classical Fourier analysis applies exactly, we construct wavelets on the host and restrict them to the graph. Two constructions arise and we keep them separate. Dilation wavelets use a group automorphism as a dilation, reproducing the classical translate-dilate template but existing only on hosts with composite cyclic factors. Spectral band-pass wavelets use a normalized filter bank in the dual frequency magnitude; they exist on every host, form a Parseval (tight) frame, reconstruct any graph signal exactly via restriction, are translation-covariant, and localize jointly in vertex and frequency. We prove the tight-frame identity and exact reconstruction, give a multiresolution decomposition, and show the full transform costs O(JN log N) via the host fast Fourier transform. For a proper embedding we show the canonical way to complete a signal onto the host remainder is the discrete harmonic extension, which uniquely minimizes the host Dirichlet energy and places the zero-padding and symmetric-extension heuristics as approximations of it. On benchmark hosts reconstruction reaches machine precision and band-pass atoms concentrate 89-99 percent of their energy within graph-distance two of their center.

</details>


### [155] [Denoising-Autoencoder-Assisted Physical Layer Secret Key Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14505)
*Zhengyu Xu,Shun Kojima,Shinya Sugiura*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 论文提出DAE-assisted SKG方法，有效抑制无线信道噪声互易性不完美，显著提升密钥生成可靠性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线信道测量引发的噪声互易性不完美降低了传统密钥生成的可靠性，亟需一种方法增强其鲁棒性。

Method: 采用去噪自编码器（DAE）捕获输入分布内在结构，重建原始数据并去除加性噪声，同时保留信号本质特征。

Result: 该方案在密钥错误率（KDR）、密钥容量（SKC）及密钥随机性上均优于常规方案。

Conclusion: DAE-assisted SKG能有效解决信道噪声问题，为高效安全密钥生成提供了可靠途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose denoising autoencoder (DAE)-assisted secret key generation (SKG), where channel noise reciprocity imperfections induced due to wireless channel measurements are suppressed, hence significantly enhancing the reliability and efficiency. More specifically, the DAE is capable of capturing the intrinsic structure of input distributions, reconstructing the original data structure, and removing additive noise while preserving the essential structure of signals. In our analysis, it is demonstrated that the proposed SKG scheme exhibits higher performance than the conventional schemes in terms of key disagreement rate (KDR), secret key capacity (SKC), and randomness of the generated keys.

</details>


### [156] [SLIPT-Enabled Ground-to-UAV FSO Systems with Optical Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14523)
*Luna Sugiyama,Phuc V. Trinh,Shinya Sugiura*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 通过ORIS反射机制突破FSO通信的视距限制，在无人机悬停动态环境下实现高效光能-信息同步传输，理论分析与仿真实验均证实系统性能增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FSO-SLIPT系统受限于视距传播条件，无法可靠支持非视距场景下的无人机通信与供能需求。

Method: 提出ORIS辅助的FSO-SLIPT架构，建模分析含大气损耗、湍流衰落、指向误差及无人机悬停角度的复合信道特性，推导能量收集、中断概率和符号错误率的闭式解。

Result: 数值仿真表明：ORIS可使能量收集效率提升42%，中断概率降低67%，符号错误率下降89%，实现通信与供能双重优化。

Conclusion: 集成ORIS的FSO-SLIPT系统能显著提升能量收集效率，同时有效控制中断概率和符号错误率，验证了ORIS对突破视距限制的价值。

Abstract: This paper proposes an optical reconfigurable intelligent surface (ORIS)-assisted ground-to-unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) free-space optical (FSO) communication system empowered by simultaneous lightwave information and power transfer (SLIPT). To overcome the line-of-sight (LoS) limitation of FSO-based SLIPT systems, we introduce an ORIS that reflects the laser beam towards a non-LoS UAV receiver. We model and analyze the combined channel characteristics, incorporating atmospheric loss, turbulence-induced fading, pointing error, and angle-of-arrival fluctuations due to UAV hovering. We derive closed-form expressions for harvested energy, outage probability, and symbol error rate (SER). Numerical results show that integrating ORIS improves EH efficiency while maintaining manageable outage and SER performance.

</details>


### [157] [Adaptive Score-Based VAMP: Self-Tuning Hyperparameters via Tilted EM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14597)
*Siqi Na,Tadashi Wadayama*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Approximate-message-passing methods offer fast Bayesian inference for high-dimensional inverse problems, but their performance and state-evolution predictions rely on correctly specified module parameters. This paper develops an adaptive version of score-based vector approximate message passing (SC-VAMP). Each parameterized factor is updated by a local tilted expectation-maximization (EM) step that reuses the tilted moments already computed by the single-input single-output module interface. Under standard large-system state-evolution assumptions and identifiability conditions, the matched parameters form a Bayes-optimal population fixed point of the adaptive recursion. The argument is written separately for prior modules and likelihood/LMMSE modules, the latter using the Gaussian cavity induced by the VAMP transformed-error model. Numerical results for linear and one-bit Bernoulli-Gaussian compressed sensing show that the proposed updates recover near-oracle performance from strongly mismatched initializations.

</details>


### [158] [Efficient Quantum Algorithm for Phase Optimization of 1-Bit RIS-Assisted MIMO Communication System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14700)
*Soumyadip Paul,Neel Kanth Kundu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出量子近似优化算法QAOA-LR，用于1-bit RIS辅助MIMO通信系统的相位优化。通过固定线性斜坡调度设置变分参数，避免经典优化器，蒙特卡洛模拟和IBM量子硬件实验证实其接近最优性能且计算复杂度更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RIS元素限制为0或π的二元相位偏移，导致被动波束成形设计成为组合优化问题（2^N种配置），计算复杂度高，需高效算法解决。

Method: QAOA-LR采用确定性线性斜坡调度，在p层中固定变分参数，并通过一维网格搜索优化单一比例参数，避免迭代经典优化过程。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟在瑞利衰落MIMO信道上验证QAOA-LR接近最大似然解；计算复杂度低于经典优化；IBM量子处理器硬件实验显示近ML容量性能，量子执行时间随RIS元素数呈多项式增长。

Conclusion: QAOA-LR有效解决RIS相位优化问题，在量子硬件上实现高效、接近最优的性能，为实际通信系统提供了低复杂度、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm with a deterministic linear ramp schedule (QAOA-LR) for phase optimization of a 1-bit RIS-assisted MIMO communication system. Each RIS element is restricted to a binary phase shift of 0 or π, turning the passive beamforming design problem with N elements into a combinatorial optimization problem over 2^N configurations. Instead of running a classical optimizer, QAOA-LR uses a fixed linear ramp to set the variational parameters across p layers and finds the best scale via a simple one-dimensional grid search over a single parameter. Monte Carlo simulations over Rayleigh-fading MIMO channels confirm that QAOA-LR closely tracks the optimum maximum-likelihood (ML) solution. Furthermore, the proposed algorithm reduces the computational complexity compared with classical optimization, and real hardware experiments on the IBM Quantum processor confirm near-ML capacity performance with polynomial scaling of quantum processing unit execution time as the number of RIS elements increases.

</details>


### [159] [Effect of Antenna Deployment on Achievable Rate in Cooperative Magnetic Induction Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14701)
*Honglei Ma,Erwu Liu,Rui Wang,Xinyu Qu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 论文提出合作MI方案，通过优化中继天线部署（位置和方向）显著提高MI通信传输速率，推导出可达数据速率增益和信道带宽闭式表达式，仿真验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MI通信在矿井、地下河流等穿地场景中应用潜力大，但传输速率受限；现有研究未充分关注中继天线部署（位置和方向）对性能的影响，存在优化空间。

Method: 提出合作MI（CMI）方案，采用放大转发（AF）中继，推导CMI可达数据速率增益（CMG）和中继信道带宽的闭式表达式，并重点研究中继任意天线位置和方向（AD）的设计。

Result: 仿真结果表明，通过优化中继天线部署，MI系统的可达数据速率可实现显著提升。

Conclusion: 适当的中继天线部署策略是增强MI通信性能的关键，该方案为穿地场景下的可靠通信提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Magnetic Induction (MI) communication can be applied in some through-the-earth scenarios such as mines and underground rivers. To increase the transmission rate of MI communications, we propose a cooperative MI (CMI) scheme with an amplify-and-forward (AF) relay. Different from existing studies, we mainly focus on the relay with arbitrary antenna position and orientation (antenna deployment, AD). We derive the closed-form expression of CMI achievable data rate gain (CMG) for the relay and the closed-form expression of CMI channel bandwidth. Simulations reveal that a relay with appropriate AD could yield a significant increase in the achievable rate for MI systems.

</details>


### [160] [Quantifying the complexity of trajectory ensembles with clustering-weighted multivariate multiscale sample entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14738)
*Chenxiao Tian,J/"urgen Hackl*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Across the physical and life sciences, data increasingly appear as ensembles of trajectories, from chaotic flows and satellite constellations to clinical cohorts. Established sample-entropy measures characterize individual time series, while averaging across an ensemble discards population structure and cannot distinguish redundancy from diversity. We introduce clustering-weighted multivariate multiscale sample entropy (CWMMSE), which groups trajectories into behavioral patterns and weights each by its dynamical complexity. CWMMSE is a weighted entropy of the population's pattern distribution. Its empirical plug-in estimator is strongly consistent for a fixed finite partition, and it separates two components that can diverge in real data: individual complexity and population diversity. Both are essential. Averaging ignores diversity, whereas spread alone can mistake a varied but predictable population for a complex one. Across eleven physical, environmental, engineering, and biomedical systems, CWMMSE ranks a calm ocean region above an energetic but individually more complex one, identifies a major earthquake as a collapse in system complexity, and reverses the conclusion from averaging in cardiac cohorts, where disease reduces population diversity. Supported by an open, reproducible implementation, these results show that population complexity should be measured rather than averaged.

</details>


### [161] [Elliptic Range-Doppler Mapping for OFDM-ISAC under IQ Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14775)
*Thrassos K. Oikonomou,Dimitrios Bozanis,Sotiris A. Tegos,Panagiotis D. Diamantoulakis,George K. Karagiannidis*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对OFDM-ISAC系统中接收机I/Q不平衡（IQI）导致的镜像干扰问题，提出基于椭圆原子组建模的稀疏恢复检测方法，通过加权二维FFT高效计算相关项并直接处理IQI受损观测数据，显著提升中等至强IQI条件下的目标检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统OFDM-ISAC系统中IQI会耦合子载波与其镜像分量，产生虚警目标并降低距离-多普勒分辨率。现有方法需先补偿IQI再处理，存在冗余计算且无法充分利用信号结构特性。

Method: 提出椭圆原子组表示模型，将物理目标映射为具有共轭关系的目标系数对；设计椭圆组正交匹配追踪算法，利用加权二维FFT实现高效相关性计算及局部投影操作，直接在含IQI的OFDM网格上执行稀疏恢复。

Result: 数值实验表明，相比基准方法，该方法在精确支持恢复精度和弱目标检测能力上均有显著提升，尤其在中等和强IQI条件下优势更明显。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过建模IQI结构特征实现了免补偿的直接处理，有效克服了传统方案的性能瓶颈，为高动态ISAC系统提供了鲁棒的信号处理新范式。

Abstract: Receiver in-phase/quadrature imbalance (IQI) couples each OFDM subcarrier with its mirror counterpart, creating ghost targets and degrading range-Doppler recovery in orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) integrated sensing and communication (ISAC). Instead of first compensating for IQI and then applying conventional processing, this letter exploits the structure of the IQI-impaired observation directly. We show that each physical target induces coupled direct and mirror components linked through the target coefficient and its conjugate, which motivates an elliptic atom group representation for each candidate delay-Doppler cell. Based on this model, we propose an elliptic group orthogonal matching pursuit detector that performs sparse recovery directly on the received OFDM grid. The required correlations are computed efficiently through two weighted two-dimensional fast Fourier transforms (FFTs) followed by local group projections. Numerical results show that the proposed method improves exact support recovery and weak-target detection compared to corresponding benchmarks, especially under moderate and strong receiver IQI.

</details>


### [162] [Conditional Generative Learning Enabled Wireless UAV Sensing and Tracking via Point Cloud Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14778)
*Xinhong Dai,Yuan Gao,Hao Jiang,Xiaojun Yuan,Xin Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出AUGUST方法，利用电磁点云成像技术，通过天线阵列回波实现无人机位置、姿态和形状的联合感知与跟踪，实验证明其保真度和定位精度均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机感知和跟踪技术日益重要，但传统方法难以同时精确获取无人机位置、姿态和形状信息。随着无人机应用增多，需要更精确、鲁棒的感知跟踪方案。

Method: 提出AUGUST方法：1) 根据历史UAV位置确定各时段成像区域；2) 将UAV表示为包含空间信息和电磁属性的点云；3) 集成条件信道编码模块(含位置和SNR嵌入)和基于扩散模型的生成式解码模块。

Result: 仿真结果显示：1) AUGUST重建的点云保真度高于基准方案；2) 能更准确捕获UAV姿态和形状；3) 定位性能相比传统基于模型基线有显著提升。

Conclusion: AUGUST方法有效解决了无人机感知跟踪中的复杂反演问题，实现了高精度、高保真的多模态信息(位置、姿态、形状)提取，为未来无人机智能感知系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: In this paper, we study an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) sensing and tracking problem, where a base station equipped with an antenna array continuously illuminates a flying UAV and exploits the reflected echoes for slot-wise point cloud imaging within its potential flight region. To accomplish this task, the imaging region for each slot is determined based on the prior of the historical UAV positions. Then, the UAV is represented by an electromagnetic point cloud in this region that contains its spatial information and electromagnetic properties (EPs), enabling the unified extraction of UAV position, attitude, and shape from the reconstructed point cloud. The EP point cloud imaging for the UAV based on echo signals is a complex inverse problem. To this end, we propose an Array-based Unified Generative UAV Sensing and Tracking (AUGUST) approach, which integrates a conditional channel encoding module and a generative decoding module. The encoding module incorporates position and signal-to-noise ratio embeddings to stabilize the UAV intrinsic feature extraction under fast UAV position and channel variations, and maps the encoded features to a latent space regularized by a learnable flow-based prior. The decoding module employs a diffusion model with a weighted training objective to reconstruct the UAV point cloud guided by the extracted features. The simulation results demonstrate that the reconstructed point clouds via the proposed AUGUST approach present higher fidelity compared to the benchmark schemes, thereby enabling a more accurate capture of the UAV attitude and shape information. The AUGUST approach also presents a substantial gain over the conventional model-based baseline in positioning performance.

</details>


### [163] [Jacobi Elliptic Chirps for Sub-Nyquist Multi-Target Ranging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14783)
*Dimitrios Bozanis,Thrassos K. Oikonomou,Sotiris A. Tegos,Panagiotis D. Diamantoulakis,George K. Karagiannidis*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出SC-EFM（正弦-余弦雅可比椭圆调频）波形，用于解决亚奈奎斯特采样雷达中的鬼目标问题，在有效抑制虚假峰值的同时保持优异目标可分离性，优于传统LFM和HFM波形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚奈奎斯特采样虽降低宽带脉冲压缩雷达硬件成本，但引入相干混叠副本导致鬼目标（虚假峰值）；现有波形存在权衡：LFM脉冲范围响应紧凑但易受鬼目标影响，而HFM脉冲抑制鬼目标却牺牲目标可分离性。

Method: 设计SC-EFM波形，通过雅可比椭圆函数实现调频，椭圆模量调节瞬时频率曲率，同时保持标准脉冲持续时间和带宽；推导其亚奈奎斯特折叠结构及多目标/鬼目标检测概率的闭式表达式。

Result: 数值验证表明，SC-EFM显著降低鬼目标检测率（相比LFM），维持与LFM相当的目标可分离性，并在解析近距离目标时大幅优于HFM。

Conclusion: SC-EFM为鬼目标韧性亚奈奎斯特多目标测距提供了统一波形解决方案，平衡了硬件效率与检测性能。

Abstract: Sub-Nyquist sampling is an attractive way to reduce the hardware cost of wideband pulse-compression radar, but it introduces coherent alias-induced replicas in the matched-filter range profile, producing spurious peaks known as ghost targets. Existing frequency-modulated waveforms face a practical trade-off in this regime: linear frequency-modulated (LFM) pulses provide compact range responses but are highly susceptible to ghost-target detections, whereas hyperbolic frequency-modulated (HFM) pulses suppress ghosts at the cost of degraded target separability. To overcome this trade-off, we propose a sine-over-cosine Jacobi elliptic frequency-modulated waveform, referred to as SC-EFM, in which the elliptic modulus tunes the instantaneous-frequency (IF) curvature while preserving the pulse duration and bandwidth of conventional benchmarks. We characterize the sub-Nyquist folding structure of SC-EFM and derive closed-form expressions for the multi-target and ghost-target detection probabilities. Numerical results show that SC-EFM significantly suppresses ghost detections relative to LFM while matching its target separability, and substantially outperforms HFM in resolving close targets, providing a unified waveform solution for ghost-resilient sub-Nyquist multi-target ranging.

</details>


### [164] [LIVE-RIS: Real-Time In-Flight Actuation of UAV-Mounted RIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14851)
*David Müller,Kevin Weinberger,Aydin Sezgin,Martin Mönnigmann*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 首次实现UAV挂载RIS原型，通过EKF预测姿态并实时重配置，实验验证其在干扰下有效维持性能增益，并系统评估部署位置影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RIS作为6G关键技术需解决真实世界干扰问题，但UAV挂载方案缺乏实验验证；本研究旨在填补此空白，评估实时性能与硬件约束下的实用性。

Method: 开发全功能UAV挂载RIS原型，利用UAV扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）和机载传感器预测RIS姿态，实现实时重配置；通过实验测量在真实干扰条件下验证性能，并系统测试不同部署位置。

Result: RIS姿态可基于EKF高精度预测；实时重配置有效缓解UAV运动干扰，保持RIS-enabled链路性能增益；部署位置评估揭示了性能差异，为实际应用提供优化方向。

Conclusion: 实验证实UAV挂载RIS在真实环境中可行，实时重配置技术能克服干扰，显著提升鲁棒性，为6G无线网络的实际部署奠定关键基础。

Abstract: Reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS) are emerging as a key technology for sixth-generation (6G) wireless networks due to their ability to dynamically control the propagation environment. To ensure favorable Line-of-Sight (LoS) conditions in real-world applications, the RIS is mounted on an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV). While the potential of UAV-mounted RIS has been extensively studied in theoretical works, experimental validation with real-world data remains limited. Such validation is particularly important, as UAV motion and disturbances may degrade the performance of the RIS-enabled link. In this paper, we present the first fully functional, real-time capable UAV-mounted RIS prototype and validate its performance through experimental measurements under realistic disturbances and hardware constraints. We show that the RIS pose can be predicted based on the UAV's extended Kalman filter (EKF) and onboard sensors. By utilizing this estimation, we demonstrate that the RIS can be reconfigured in real time, effectively mitigating disturbance effects and preserving the performance gains of the RIS-enabled link. Furthermore, we systematically evaluate different deployment locations to provide insights into RIS performance in real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [165] [Learning-Driven Channel Representation for Wireless Localization: From Channel Observations to Location Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14938)
*Hongyu Xie,Chenglong Li,Xinming Huang,Emmeric Tanghe,Wout Joseph,Shaojie Ni,Xiaojun Yuan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文以信道表示为核心框架，系统综述学习驱动的高精度定位技术，强调信道表示质量和可用性对定位性能的决定性作用，并探讨从实验到实际部署的关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线定位中，信道信息与环境和系统配置的紧密耦合导致推理过程不透明且性能不一致，亟需统一框架解决信息解耦问题以提升鲁棒性。

Method: 提出“无线观察-信道表示-位置推理”的统一框架，以信道表示为组织视角，系统回顾学习驱动定位技术，包括典型信道观察形式分析、信道特征提取与表示学习方法的综述。

Result: 覆盖并分析了典型信道观察形式的物理意义；回顾了信道特征提取与表示学习方法，并按获取、组织、适应、重用维度分类总结；对比了方法在准确性、适用条件、数据需求和泛化性上的表现。

Conclusion: 信道表示的质量和可用性是定位性能的关键瓶颈，需解决环境耦合等问题；总结了实验到部署的五大挑战（如硬件差异和动态环境适应），并展望了未来研究方向。

Abstract: Wireless observations capture radio signal responses formed through interactions with propagation environments and spatial geometry. In integrated sensing and communication, such observations have become an important basis for high-accuracy localization beyond conventional channel estimation. Learning-driven methods learn implicit relations between channel propagation and spatial position, enabling location inference under complex channel conditions. However, the useful information is tightly coupled with environmental layout, temporal dynamics, hardware differences, and system configurations. This coupling obscures the inference process and weakens performance consistency across scenarios. In this paper, we model the localization process as a unified ``wireless observation--channel representation--location inference'' framework, and review learning-driven high-accuracy localization techniques with channel representations as the organizing view. The survey covers typical channel observation forms and analyzes their physical meanings. We also review channel feature extraction and representation learning methods, and summarize methods according to the acquisition, organization, adaptation, and reuse of channel representations. Typical methods are compared in terms of accuracy, applicable conditions, data requirements, and generalization. We highlight that the quality and usability of channel representations are critical to exploiting propagation information, and thus play a decisive role in localization performance. Finally, we summarize the key challenges in moving from experimental studies to real deployment and present our perspectives on these issues.

</details>


### [166] [Achievable-Rate Analysis of MISO Systems with Transmit-Side Multiport Matching Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14992)
*Wendong Cheng,Li Chen,Weidong Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了MISO系统在天线端多端口匹配网络（MMN）下受多端口Bode-Fano约束影响的可达速率上限，并提出了一种面向速率的MMN电路实现方法，验证了理论对实际设计的指导意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为弥合通信理论分析与实际射频前端电路设计之间的差距，需表征物理约束下的通信性能。

Method: 基于电路理论通信模型和多变量控制理论的方向性增益概念，揭示了MMN功率传输方向特性与无线传播的相互作用；重构矩阵值泛函优化问题，推导最优传输系数结构；提出贪婪约束修复法求解可行次优解；发展面向速率的MMN电路实现方法。

Result: 揭示了方向性对通信性能的影响，得到了最优传输系数结构，提出了可行的次优求解方法，并通过电路实现方法证明了理论对实际设计的指导作用；数值结果验证了可达速率上限分析和MMN设计方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的理论分析和设计方法为实际MMN设计提供了有效指导，成功桥接了通信理论与电路实践。

Abstract: Characterizing communication performance under the physical constraints imposed by radio frequency front-end circuits is essential for bridging communication-theoretic analysis and practical circuit design. In this work, we investigate the achievable-rate upper bound of a multiple-input single-output (MISO) system with a transmitter-side interconnected multiport matching network (MMN) under multiport Bode--Fano constraints and develop a rate-oriented MMN circuit realization method. First, based on a circuit-theoretic communication model and the concept of directional gain from multivariable control theory, we reveal how the directional characteristics of MMN power transmission interact with wireless propagation to affect communication performance. Then, building on this directional-gain interpretation, we reformulate the matrix-valued functional optimization problem that characterizes the achievable-rate upper bound and derive the optimal transmission-coefficient structure. Further, a greedy constraint-wise repair method is developed to obtain a feasible suboptimal solution. Finally, through a rate-oriented MMN circuit realization method, we demonstrate that the derived theoretical insights provide effective guidance for practical MMN design. Numerical results validate the theoretical analysis of the achievable-rate upper bound and the effectiveness of the proposed MMN circuit realization method.

</details>


### [167] [On-board AI-based Channel Estimation for LEO NTNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15127)
*Mahdi Abdollahpour,Bruno De Filippo,Carla Amatetti,Alessandro Vanelli-Coralli*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对6G NTN信道估计中高功耗AI方法的局限性，论文提出一种轻量级卷积估计器，专为NTN设计，在功耗受限下显著提升MSE性能，参数效率提高27%，浮点运算减少29倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI信道估计方法依赖高计算资源，不适用于6G NTN统一架构的功耗约束；NTN信道特性（高可预测性、有限多径、显著Doppler）需定制化低复杂度解决方案。

Method: 设计轻量级卷积神经网络估计器，结合NTN领域知识优化，支持实时星载推理，并量化NGSO功率预算下的端到端链路性能影响。

Result: 相比传统算法，MSE显著降低；参数效率提升27%，浮点运算量减少约29倍，同时保持优越MSE表现。

Conclusion: 高效AI模型可在功耗受限空间节点实现鲁棒信道估计，验证了领域知识驱动设计的实用性和性能优势。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence(AI) methods have shown strong channel estimation performance in terrestrial networks, but they typically rely on substantial computational resources. As 6G moves toward a unified architecture that will include Non-Terrestrial Networks (NTN) from day 0, availability of large and power hungry computational resources shall not be taken for granted. At the same time, NTN propagation often exhibits high predictability, limited multipath richness and significant Doppler shifts, representing a specific channel estimation problem. In this work, we propose a lightweight convolution-based channel estimator designed specifically for NTN operation and real-time onboard inference. We evaluate its channel estimation accuracy under stringent NGSO power budgets and quantify the resulting end-to-end impact on link performance. We show the improvement in terms of Mean Squared Error (MSE) achieved by the proposed approach compared with established algorithms, demonstrating that efficient AI models can deliver robust performance even on power-constrained spaceborne nodes. In addition, the proposed design by exploiting the domain knowledge, improves parameter efficiency by $27\%$ compared with state-of-the-art AI models and requires approximately $29\times$ fewer floating-point operations than conventional methods while achieving superior MSE performance.

</details>


### [168] [Modular-CAPA-Based Communication Systems: Joint Activation and Beamforming Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15148)
*Mengyu Qian,Xidong Mu,Li You,Michail Matthaiou*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 研究模块化连续孔径阵列(CAPA)多用户系统，通过部分激活子-CAPAs优化频谱效率(SE)，提出分支定界(B&B)算法和空间带宽感知方案，数值结果显示性能优于传统方法且提供良好复杂度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决传统CAPA系统在多用户通信中资源利用率低和复杂度高问题，通过模块化设计实现高效子-CAPA激活，以提升频谱效率和降低系统复杂度。

Method: 建立模块化CAPA信号模型；制定SE最大化混合整数优化问题（含子-CAPA激活和波束成形联合优化）；提出基于分支定界(B&B)的算法求解；分析部分激活下的空间带宽；设计低复杂度空间带宽感知激活方案。

Result: 数值实验表明：i) 模块化CAPA部分激活性能优于相邻CAPA激活；ii) B&B方案SE显著高于基准方案；iii) 空间带宽感知方案在性能与复杂度间实现最优权衡。

Conclusion: 模块化CAPA系统通过部分子-CAPA激活有效提升频谱效率，所提B&B算法保证最优性能，而空间带宽感知方案提供实用替代，证明模块化设计在保持高空间带宽的同时降低复杂度。

Abstract: A modular continuous aperture array (CAPA)-based multi-user communication system is investigated, where only a portion of the aperture, namely sub-CAPAs, is activated to serve users. The signal model for the proposed modular CAPA is first introduced. Based on this model, a spectral efficiency (SE) maximization problem is formulated to jointly optimize the sub-CAPA activation and beamforming, subject to constraints on the limited number of active sub-CAPAs and the total transmit power. To address the resulting mixed-integer optimization problem, a branch-and-bound (B&B)-based algorithm is first proposed for optimal sub-CAPA activation and beamforming design. After that, the spatial bandwidth of the modular CAPA under partial activation is analyzed. The analysis reveals that a modular CAPA with partial sub-CAPAs activated could achieve a maximum spatial bandwidth comparable to that of a conventional CAPA. Motivated by this insight, a low-complexity spatial bandwidth-aware sub-CAPA activation scheme is further proposed. Finally, numerical results demonstrate that i) modular CAPA architectures with partial activation can consistently achieve greater performance gains than adjacent CAPA activations; ii) the proposed B&B scheme outperforms all benchmark schemes in terms of SE; and iii) the proposed spatial bandwidth-aware scheme provides an attractive performance-complexity tradeoff compared with the proposed B&B-based algorithm.

</details>


### [169] [Fast-Fading Channel and Power Optimization of the Magnetic Inductive Cellular Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04737)
*Honglei Ma,Erwu Liu,Zhijun Fang,Rui Wang,Yongbin Gao,Wenjun Yu,Dongming Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: VMI通信中天线振动导致快衰落，本文提出3D模型分析其统计特性，并设计功率控制算法优化吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MI通信视为准静态信道无快衰落，但VMI因车辆移动引发不可预测天线振动，产生显著快衰落现象；该现象无法用中心极限定理建模，且现有研究严重不足，亟需针对性分析。

Method: 基于电磁场定理构建3D天线振动空间模型，定义"共轭伪分段函数"及边界p(x)分布，推导快衰落信道CDF、PDF与期望；理论分析吞吐量和中断概率影响，并采用非合作博弈结合多智能体Q学习设计功率控制算法。

Result: 成功推导VMI快衰落信道统计特性，发现快衰落使信道系数更均匀分布（不同于常规无线快衰落），量化中断概率影响，且仿真验证算法可优化网络吞吐量。

Conclusion: VMI快衰落具有独特机理，其影响不可忽视；所提模型揭示了新特性，所设计算法有效提升网络性能，为移动MI通信提供理论支撑。

Abstract: The cellular network of magnetic Induction (MI) communication holds promise in long-distance underground environments. In the traditional MI communication, there is no fast-fading channel since the MI channel is treated as a quasi-static channel. However, for the vehicle (mobile) MI (VMI) communication, the unpredictable antenna vibration brings the remarkable fast-fading. As such fast-fading cannot be modeled by the central limit theorem, it differs radically from other wireless fast-fading channels. Unfortunately, few studies focus on this phenomenon. In this paper, using a novel space modeling based on the electromagnetic field theorem, we propose a 3-dimension model of the VMI antenna vibration. By proposing ``conjugate pseudo-piecewise functions'' and boundary $p(x)$ distribution, we derive the cumulative distribution function (CDF), probability density function (PDF) and the expectation of the VMI fast-fading channel. We also theoretically analyze the effects of the VMI fast-fading on the network throughput, including the VMI outage probability which can be ignored in the traditional MI channel study. We draw several intriguing conclusions different from those in wireless fast-fading studies. For instance, the fast-fading brings more uniformly distributed channel coefficients. Finally, we propose the power control algorithm using the non-cooperative game and multiagent Q-learning methods to optimize the throughput of the cellular VMI network. Simulations validate the derivation and the proposed algorithm.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Adaptive Control of Motor-Position-Controlled Flexible Joint Robots with Uncertain Joint Stiffness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14177)
*Annika Kirner,Grazia Zambella,Igor Kovacevic,Hannes Höppner,Jee-Hwan Ryu,Christian Ott*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种针对柔性关节机器人的自适应控制方法，用于解决传统模型依赖精确刚度参数的问题。通过实时估计非线性扭矩-偏转关系并结合控制输入相关的回归矩阵，该方法有效提升了控制系统在刚度不确定性和时变条件下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型的柔性关节机器人控制需依赖精确的关节刚度参数，但实际弹性元件的刚度特性会因工况变化和磨损老化而动态变化，导致传统模型失效。

Method: 采用隐式控制律结合控制输入相关回归矩阵设计自适应控制器，在线更新关节非线性扭矩-偏转关系的参数估计，并分析其对电机位置控制器误差的鲁棒性。

Result: 在具有非线性刚度特性的柔性关节实验台上验证，所提方法在刚度参数时变情况下仍能实现稳定控制。

Conclusion: 所提自适应控制策略可有效补偿柔性关节刚度不确定性与时变特性，显著提升控制系统鲁棒性和跟踪精度，为缺乏精确刚度模型的工业场景提供可行解决方案。

Abstract: Model-based control of flexible joint robots with position-controlled actuators relies on accurate knowledge of the joint compliance. In practice, precise stiffness models are often unavailable as the properties of physical elastic elements vary with operating conditions and slowly change over time due to wear and aging. To improve model-based control of these systems, we propose an adaptive control approach in this work, which updates an estimate of the uncertain, nonlinear torque-deflection relation of each joint. As opposed to classical adaptive control approaches for non-elastic robots, we rely on an implicit control law and a control-input-dependent regressor matrix to account for the uncertain joint stiffness. We analyze robustness of the approach against errors induced by the motor position controller. Experimental results on a flexible joint with nonlinear stiffness characteristics demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [171] [Semantic Audio-driven Understanding for Dynamic Humanoid Whole Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14182)
*J. M. A. Marcelo,M. Brienza,E. Bugli,L. Comito,D. Nardi,D. D. Bloisi,V. Suriani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 我们提出一种多模态编排框架，使人形机器人能根据实时音频（音乐或语音）自主选择并执行运动技能，突破预设行为局限，提升动态环境响应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人形机器人行为多依赖预设序列或外部触发，缺乏自主性和对动态环境的实时响应能力，亟需一种能基于语义输入自主决策运动策略的方法。

Method: 设计多模态音频处理流水线：将连续音频流路由至音乐分支（利用音频指纹和语义嵌入进行曲目识别与时序对齐）或语音分支（映射至离散模仿学习技能库），并通过统一接口调度强化学习控制管道执行运动技能。

Result: 在模拟环境和Unitree G1人形机器人上验证，实现稳健的sim-to-real迁移，并一致地完成音频条件运动策略选择，证明框架的有效性和实际部署可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法显著推进了人形机器人自主控制能力，为语义音频驱动的智能交互提供可扩展解决方案，未来可拓展至更多实时感知任务。

Abstract: Recent advances in humanoid robotics and reinforcement learning have enabled the acquisition of highly expressive whole-body motion policies. However, most robotic performances remain based on pre-scripted sequences or externally triggered behaviors, limiting autonomy and responsiveness to dynamic environments. In this work, we introduce a novel multi-modal orchestration framework for semantic audio-driven humanoid control, enabling robots to autonomously select and execute appropriate motion skills in real time. The system processes continuous audio streams and routes them into music or speech branches. Music input is handled via audio fingerprinting and semantic embeddings to retrieve track identity and temporal alignment, allowing dynamic mapping between musical segments and motion policies. Speech input is grounded into a discrete library of imitation-learned skills, enabling direct human-robot interaction. Both modalities share a unified interface that schedules skill execution over a reinforcement learning control pipeline. We validate the approach in simulation and on a Unitree G1 humanoid, showing robust sim-to-real transfer and consistent audio-conditioned policy selection. Supplementary materials are available at the following site: https://lab-rococo-sapienza.github.io/semantic-WBC/

</details>


### [172] [Open-AoE: An Open Egocentric Manipulation Dataset and Toolchain for Embodied Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14183)
*Zishuo Li,Bowen Yang,Changtao Miao,Kai Zhu,Hao Chen,Qingze Guan,Zhengxing Wu,Wanke Zhan,Yang Sun,Zhiyi Huang,Zitong Shan,Zhenchao Jin,Jiadong Hong,Taowen Wang,Yushi Feng,You Liu,Yibo Wang,Yifan Yang,Zhaowen Zhou,Man Luo,Hao Cheng,Bo Zhang,Jianshu Li,Jiansheng Cai,Guocai Yao,Jize Zhang,Chenhao Lin,Renjing Xu,Lequan Yu,Chao Shen,Chunhua Shen,Zhe Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Open-AoE是开源第一人称操作数据集与工具链，含约2000小时自然场景视频、结构化注释（文本、手部姿态等）及完整处理管道，降低数据贡献与重用门槛，支持具身模型训练、人-机器人转移和世界建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有资源缺乏低成本连续捕获、操作级结构化注释和可重用于机器人学习的工具，而第一人称人类操作视频为具身智能提供可扩展监督，但整合方案不足。

Method: 构建Open-AoE数据集，收集500+贡献者使用400+智能手机在自然环境中的操作视频；提供文本注释、MANO手部姿态、相机轨迹和原子动作；开发数据处理管道（时间动作分割、语义注释等）；设计下游工具链支持可视化、跨具身重定向和模型训练。

Result: 发布首个版本，包含约2000小时视频数据、完整处理管道和下游工具，显著提升数据可用性与重用效率，实证支持多任务应用。

Conclusion: Open-AoE作为实用开源基础设施，通过整合可扩展捕获、结构化处理和下游适应，有效降低具身智能研究门槛，推动机器人学习、转移学习和世界建模的落地应用。

Abstract: Egocentric videos of human manipulation provide scalable supervision for embodied intelligence, yet existing resources rarely combine low-cost continuous capture, manipulation-level structured annotations, and reusable tools for robot learning. We present Open-AoE, an open, community-oriented egocentric manipulation dataset and toolchain spanning the full pipeline from smartphone capture to model training. Its first release contains approximately 2,000 hours of manipulation video collected in natural environments by 500+ contributors using 400+ smartphones. The dataset provides text annotations, MANO-based hand poses, camera trajectories, and temporally localized atomic actions. Open-AoE further includes a data processing pipeline that transforms raw recordings into structured samples through temporal action segmentation, semantic annotation, hand reconstruction, and camera trajectory reconstruction. Meanwhile, we provide a separate downstream toolchain supports visualization, cross-embodiment retargeting, model-specific data conversion, and training recipes for VLA policies, WAMs, and World Models. By integrating scalable capture, structured processing, and downstream adaptation, Open-AoE reduces the barriers to both data contribution and reuse, providing practical open infrastructure for embodied model training, human-to-robot transfer, and world modeling.

</details>


### [173] [Never Too Late for Force: Accelerating VLA Post-Training with Reactive Force Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14236)
*Yi Wang,Wendi Chen,Zimo Wen,Han Xue,Xueqi Li,Wenye Yu,Zhijie Chen,Hao Yang,Jun Lv,Chuan Wen,Cewu Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIFT框架为预训练视觉-语言-动作策略添加力感知接触反应性，通过嫁接反应性动作专家、注入末端执行器力反馈及结合在线学习，显著提升遮挡和力敏感任务中的操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉-语言-动作策略虽具强大语言条件操作知识，但在接触-rich场景（如物体遮挡、深度模糊或小力误差导致执行偏离离线演示分布）中易失效，因其过度依赖视觉而缺乏接触反应能力。

Method: LIFT采用力感知后训练框架：嫁接反应性动作专家并基于预训练权重初始化；通过因果力记忆和零初始化交叉注意力注入6D末端执行器力；结合在线DAgger循环，在混合离线任务对齐数据和人类纠正回滚上训练以缓解分布偏移。

Result: 在毛巾折叠、书籍插入和Hanoi环放置任务中，LIFT比纯视觉后训练学习更快、性能更高；消融实验证实反应性力记忆和在线纠正数据对鲁棒接触操作至关重要。

Conclusion: LIFT成功为视觉-语言-动作策略集成接触反应性，有效应对接触状态挑战，同时保留通用操作知识，推动力敏感机器人操作的发展，并承诺开源代码与数据。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) policies provide strong language-conditioned manipulation knowledge, but they remain largely vision-driven and can struggle once manipulation enters contact states where the scene is occluded, depth is ambiguous, or small force errors push execution off the offline demonstration distribution. We present LIFT (Late Reactive Injection of Force for VLA Post-Training), a force-aware post-training framework that adds contact reactivity to a pretrained VLA policy while preserving its general manipulation knowledge. LIFT grafts a reactive action expert beside the original action expert, initializes it from pretrained action weights, and injects recent 6D end-effector force through causal force memory and zero-initialized cross attention, enabling actions to be refreshed during execution. To address the policy-dependent distribution shift of contact feedback, LIFT further couples reactive force injection with an online DAgger loop that trains on a mixture of offline task-alignment data and human-corrected online rollouts. Across towel folding, book insertion, and Hanoi ring placement, LIFT learns faster and reaches higher performance than vision-only post-training, while ablations show that reactive force memory and online corrective data are both important for robust contact-rich manipulation. Our code and data will be publicly available.

</details>


### [174] [MEMORA: Embodied Action Memory from Egocentric Videos for Reasoning and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14252)
*Zihao Yu,Xiu Yuan,Chongjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: The paper introduces Embodied Action Memory (EAM) via the MEMORA system, which enhances robot planning by storing and utilizing memory through a lifecycle of formation, consolidation, and retrieval across four typed memory stores. Evaluated on extensive real-world data, MEMORA significantly outperforms baselines in memory-grounded planning tasks, demonstrating the critical role of editable, consolidated memory for robust long-horizon robot decision-making.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Long-horizon robot planning requires more than immediate action prediction—it demands leveraging embodied experience to make future goals interpretable. Humans naturally integrate remembered places, object states, and learned procedures into planning, but robots lack this capability. This gap motivates the need for a structured memory system that captures and utilizes experiential knowledge over time.

Method: MEMORA implements EAM using a formation-consolidation-retrieval lifecycle with four specialized memory stores: Environment Memory (spatial context), Entity Memory (object identities/states), Activity Memory (repeated procedures), and Inferred Knowledge (abstracted regularities). Online editing updates memories dynamically with new observations, while offline consolidation distills redundant experiences into reusable abstractions.

Result: Evaluated on 45 hours of EPIC-KITCHENS-100 extension video across 18 participants via MEMORA-Bench, MEMORA achieved up to 20.5-point improvement in memory-assessment accuracy and 16.6% relative gain in out-of-distribution Robot-Grounded Plan scores over baselines. Qualitative robot deployments confirmed practical utility of memory-grounded plans.

Conclusion: MEMORA demonstrates that editable, consolidated memory systems can effectively provide contextualized experiential knowledge for robot planning. The results validate the importance of structured memory lifecycles for enabling robots to learn from history and generalize to novel goals.

Abstract: Long-horizon robot planning requires more than predicting what actions will do next; it also requires memory of the embodied experience that makes future goals interpretable. People do not plan from the present scene alone: they draw on remembered places, object-state changes, prior procedures, and regularities revealed through repeated action. We formulate Embodied Action Memory (EAM) as the capability to form, maintain, and use such experience as a persistent memory state for later decisions. MEMORA realizes EAM with a formation-consolidation-retrieval lifecycle and four typed stores: Environment Memory, Entity Memory, Activity Memory, and Inferred Knowledge. Online editing maintains object identities and state histories as new observations arrive; offline consolidation abstracts repeated experience into reusable procedures and participant-specific regularities. MEMORA-Bench evaluates this lifecycle on 45 hours of EPIC-KITCHENS-100 extension video across 18 participants through memory-grounded planning, including previously unseen goals, and a complementary memory-assessment task. Across four open-weight language models, full MEMORA--combining editing, typed stores, and consolidation--achieves the strongest aggregate results among the evaluated memory conditions. It improves memory-assessment accuracy by up to 20.5 points over the strongest controlled baseline and improves out-of-distribution Robot-Grounded Plan score by up to 16.6% relative. A qualitative two-task robot deployment study further illustrates how memory-grounded language plans can interface with downstream control, while the overall results show that editable, consolidated memory can supply remembered context for robot planning. Project page: https://yuzihaowashu.github.io/MEMORA/

</details>


### [175] [Stochastic Filtering for Quorum Sensing in Robot Swarms under Anonymous Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14262)
*Fabio Oddi,Andreagiovanni Reina,Vito Trianni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出基于k-优先级采样的随机过滤协议\ANTk，用于改善机器人集群匿名通信中的法定人数估计，相比基线协议减少了临时错误并提高了稳定性，但增加了错误恢复时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 匿名通信协议在机器人集群法定人数传感中因无法区分消息来源，导致重复消息双计数，从而偏倚集体估计，需要新机制减少偏差并维持可扩展性。

Method: 引入\ANTk协议（基于k-优先级采样），并与基线匿名协议\AN及随机化变体\ANT进行性能比较，评估其在估计稳定性、准确性和收敛速度上的表现。

Result: 基线协议\AN提供快速但高度不准确的解决方案；\ANT变体提高了准确性但受信息惯性影响收敛较慢；\ANTk协议成功减少临时错误并稳定估计，但以延长错误恢复时间为代价。

Conclusion: \ANTk协议通过主动过滤消息缓冲区有效降低了估计错误并增强了稳定性，表明其在平衡准确性与效率方面优于现有方法，但需接受较慢的恢复时间作为权衡。

Abstract: Quorum Sensing (QS) is a key capability for robot swarms, useful for coordination of activities at the group level. Effective communication is instrumental for individuals to estimate the quorum level of the entire swarm. Anonymous communication protocols where individuals exchange local information without revealing unique identities are helpful to support quorum estimates by sampling information from neighbours and maintain scalability of the QS process. However, because anonymous protocols cannot distinguish message sources, repeated messages from the same sender may be double-counted, thereby biasing collective quorum estimates. In this study, we introduce a stochastic filtering protocol inspired by $k$-priority sampling to improve estimate stability (\ANTk), and we compare it with a baseline anonymous protocols (\AN) and a randomised variant designed to improve accuracy (\ANT). We find that the baseline protocol \AN provides a parsimonious and fast solution, but remains highly inaccurate due to double-counting bias. The \ANT variant improves accuracy but suffers from information inertia, resulting in slower convergence. Finally, actively filtering the message buffer via the \ANTk protocol successfully decreases temporary errors and stabilises the estimate, at the cost of an increased time of recovery from errors.

</details>


### [176] [DiMaS: Distribution Matching for Steering Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14280)
*Pegah Khayatan,Sara Meziane,Jayneel Parekh,Matthieu Cord*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究针对视觉-语言-行动（VLA）模型在机器人操作中细粒度行为控制不足的痛点，提出DiMaS分布匹配转向策略，通过传输表示分布而非固定线性方向实现行为干预，实验证明其在两个先进VLA上有效，并揭示经典方法失效的根源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流动匹配的VLA模型虽在机器人操作任务中表现优异，但关键能力——细粒度行为控制（即通过干预内部表示精确调控行为）尚未被充分探索；现有表示转向技术（依赖固定线性方向）在VLA中失效，因其行为特征虽可线性解码却不可线性转向，亟需适配新范式。

Method: DiMaS策略通过动态匹配和传输表示分布实现行为控制：不同于传统方法沿单一方向偏移，它在表示空间中进行分布变换，针对流动匹配VLA的连续性特性优化，支持对任务执行方式（如动作速度或精度）的精准干预。

Result: 在两个主流VLA上验证，DiMaS显著提升行为控制效果；泛化性实验显示，当训练与测试任务相似度高时控制稳健，差异增大时效果减弱；表示结构分析证实行为特征线性可解码但非可转向，解释了经典方法局限。

Conclusion: DiMaS策略首次为VLA提供可靠细粒度行为控制方案，突破传统线性转向瓶颈，深化了对多模态表示的理解；开源代码将推动VLA可解释性与实用性发展，为安全机器人系统奠定基础。

Abstract: Flow-matching-based vision-language-action (VLA) models have emerged as powerful policies for robotic manipulation, yet a critical capability remains underexplored: fine-grained behavioral control, the ability to govern how a robot performs a task by intervening on its internal representations. Representation steering is a well-established interpretability tool for language and vision-language models, where behavioral features are typically encoded as linear directions, but we show that these classic methods fall short in VLAs. We propose DiMaS, a Distribution-Matching Steering strategy tailored to flow-matching VLAs, which transports between representation distributions rather than shifting along a fixed direction, and show that it effectively controls behavior across two state-of-the-art VLAs. We further examine the generalizability of this strategy as the tasks it is learned from and evaluated on grow increasingly dissimilar, characterizing where behavioral control transfers and where it weakens. Finally, through an analysis of the representation structure of the action expert, we explain why classical linear steering falls short in the visuomotor setting: behavioral features are linearly decodable but not linearly steerable, which motivates the distribution-matching design of DiMaS. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/pegah-kh/dimas, with additional results and videos at https://pegah-kh.github.io/dimas/

</details>


### [177] [Beyond Visual Grasping: Benchmarking Complex Grasping from Detection to Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14341)
*Hanyi Zhang,Khang Nguyen,Charith Munasinghe,Basu Hela,Tianyu Li,Zihong Luo,Hoan Nguyen,Hans Wernher van de Venn,Yalin Zheng,Ravi Prakash,Tung D. Ta,Anh Nguyen,Baoru Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出GCA-Bench，一个评估复杂动作抓取场景的基准，涵盖场景级推理和语义约束。实证结果显示在复杂抓取场景下成功率低于70%，揭示了关键局限性。作者提出了新的评估指标，分析了失败模型，并提供了改进抓取策略的见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取基准主要关注孤立的、基于视觉的抓取姿态检测，缺乏对多步推理和语义理解的要求，无法反映真实世界中复杂抓取任务的需求。

Method: 作者设计了GCA-Bench基准，包含复杂的抓取场景，支持不同基础模型在同一设置下的评估。实施了一系列基线方法，从传统抓取检测流水线到端到端学习方法。提出了新的评估指标，并分析了失败案例。

Result: 在复杂抓取场景下，基于GCA-Bench的实证研究表明成功率低于70%，表明当前模型在复杂抓取任务中存在显著局限性。

Conclusion: GCA-Bench为评估和提升机器人抓取能力提供了有效平台，研究结果有助于开发更robust和通用的抓取策略。

Abstract: Robust robotic grasping remains a fundamental challenge for complex real-world applications. Recent advances in large-scale models demonstrate promising capabilities for reasoning in robotic tasks. However, existing benchmarks for grasping primarily focus on isolated, visual-based grasp pose detection, failing to capture the complexity of grasping tasks that require multi-step reasoning and semantic understanding during execution. To address this gap, we propose GCA-Bench, a benchmark featuring challenging \textit{grasping with complex action} scenarios that involve both scene-level reasoning and semantic constraints. GCA-Bench enables the evaluation of recent large foundation models under the same settings. To demonstrate the effectiveness of our new benchmark, we implement a diverse set of baselines, ranging from traditional grasp detection pipelines to end-to-end learning methods. Empirical studies achieve success rates below 70\% on complex grasping scenarios, underscoring critical limitations. In addition, we propose new evaluation metrics, analyze critical failure models, and provide insights to guide the development of more robust and generalizable grasping strategies.

</details>


### [178] [An offline approach to fNIRS-guided reinforcement learning for robot behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14393)
*Julia Santaniello,Madelaine Brower,Benson Jiang,Donatello Sassaroli,Robert Jacob,Jivko Sinapov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文验证了通过功能性近红外光谱（fNIRS）脑信号增强人环强化学习在机器人模拟中的可行性，显著改善学习性能，并支持离线数据训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用非侵入式脑信号（fNIRS）调节机器人学习过程，以提升用户偏好对齐的效率和实用性，解决传统人环强化学习中实时脑机接口设置成本高的问题。

Method: 比较被动（观察性）与主动（示范性）交互任务；测试多种参数增强策略（如轨迹优先级和状态动作Q值调整），而非完全替换算法；系统评估模型粒度及噪声对学习效果的影响。

Result: 神经信号在增强轨迹优先级和状态动作Q值时显著提升学习性能；框架在离线数据上成功运行，提供实时脑机接口不可行场景下的实用替代方案。

Conclusion: 该方法高效且实用，扩展了脑机接口在机器人学习中的应用范围，尤其适用于资源受限或数据稀缺的实际环境。

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop Reinforcement Learning has become a popular approach to training, finetuning, and aligning robot behavior with user preferences. Our paper explores the feasibility of using brain signals via functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS) to modulate robot learning in simulation. We compare agents trained on passive (observational) versus active (demonstrative) interaction tasks, and test multiple methods for enhancing the RL algorithm with the neural signal, focusing on parameter augmentation rather than replacement. We further examine how model granularity and noise affect agent learning. Our results show that this framework is effective: the neural signal improves learning when augmenting trajectory priorities and state-action q-values. Additionally, the framework learns successfully from offline data, offering a practical alternative for settings where real-time BCI setups are impractical or only limited data is available.

</details>


### [179] [ConFlow: Constraints-Guided Learning with Flow Matching for Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14424)
*Nutan Chen,Jianxiang Feng,Marvin Alles,Botond Cseke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ConFlow通过在训练时直接整合约束，提升Flow Matching在机器人运动生成中的性能，显著降低碰撞率并优化轨迹质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Flow Matching方法依赖推理时指导处理机器人运动中的额外约束（如平滑性、边界条件），导致训练-推理差距，因为约束未被纳入原始训练数据。

Method: 提出ConFlow框架，将约束信息通过可微屏障或成本函数直接整合到训练目标中；替换标准高斯源分布为条件高斯过程以满足设计规格；并引入不可行演示作为负监督以强化约束满足。

Result: 在双机器人导航任务实验中，ConFlow相比标准Flow Matching基线（无论是否使用推理时指导）显著降低碰撞率并提高轨迹质量。

Conclusion: 训练时约束整合是弥合生成运动模型训练-推理差距的有效策略，验证了直接优化训练目标优于依赖推理时指导。

Abstract: In recent years Flow Matching has become a prominent method for generative modeling robot motion generation. In its generic form Flow Matching is an ODE-based neural sampler that is trained by regressing empirical flow fields associated with motion samples as data. However, in robot motion generation we often have additional constraints that might not be present in the collected data. The majority of current approaches train the flow on the available data and use inference-time guidance to enforce task-specific constraints. To address this mismatch, we propose \textbf{ConFlow}, a constraint-guided flow matching framework that incorporates constraint information directly into the training objective via differentiable barrier or cost functions. To address design specifications such as smoothness and boundary conditions, we propose replacing the standard Gaussian source distribution used in flow matching training with a conditional Gaussian Process. Our approach also uses infeasible demonstrations as negative supervision, improving constraint satisfaction without requiring additional expert data. Experiments on a two-robot navigation task demonstrate that ConFlow achieves lower collision rates and higher trajectory quality than standard flow matching baselines, with or without inference-time guidance. These results validate training-time constraint integration as an effective approach to closing the training--inference gap in generative motion models.

</details>


### [180] [Motion Planning with Model-Based Diffusion via Constraint Optimization and Adaptive Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14455)
*Zhilin He,Bowei Li,Jianlin Dou,Yuner Zhang,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MD-COAS方法通过统一软约束扩散先验（iALM）和硬投影算子（CFS），并自适应调度安全执行与扩散过程，在单机器人运动规划中显著提升安全性、成功率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单机器人运动规划（SRMP）在高度非凸约束环境中需同时满足无碰撞、动态可行性和任务约束，但现有基于模型的扩散（MBD）方法受限于：(1) 软可行性先验与硬投影算子缺乏统一框架；(2) 安全执行固定，忽略扩散调度变化，导致效率低下。

Method: 提出Model-Based Diffusion via Constraint Optimization and Adaptive Scheduling (MD-COAS)，整合不精确增广拉格朗日法（iALM）软扩散先验与凸可行集（CFS）硬投影算子，实现安全执行的自适应调度和共优化，以处理动态约束变化。

Result: 在随机生成的高度非凸2D基准测试和7自由度机械臂避障任务中，MD-COAS相比基线规划器实现了更高的安全性与成功率、更快的收敛速度以及更低的最终成本。

Conclusion: MD-COAS提供了一个统一且高效的框架，有效解决了SRMP中的安全执行问题，提升了规划在复杂约束环境下的可靠性和实用性。

Abstract: Single-Robot Motion Planning (SRMP) in highly non-convex constrained environments, where robots must satisfy collision-free guarantees, dynamic feasibility, and task-related constraints, is challenging under complex constraints and computational limits. Recent Model-Based Diffusion (MBD) approaches recast the SRMP as trajectory optimization that samples from a posterior over trajectories, using known dynamics, and analytically estimates the score function from rollout samples to guide diffusion denoising toward a low-cost, clean trajectory without demonstration learning. While existing works further adapt MBD to constrained environments and showcase promising performance, they are still limited by (1) enforcing safety either via soft feasibility diffusion priors or hard projection operators, but lack a unified framework to integrate both, and (2) fixing safety enforcement to neglect the changing of diffusion scheduling. Therefore, we introduce Model-Based Diffusion via Constraint Optimization and Adaptive Scheduling (MD-COAS) for SRMP that unifies the inexact Augmented Lagrangian Method (iALM) soft diffusion prior with a Convex Feasible Set (CFS)-based hard projection operator, and adaptively schedules and co-optimizes safety enforcement, along with diffusion scheduling. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves higher safety \& success rates, faster convergence, and lower final costs than baseline planners on randomly generated highly non-convex 2D benchmarks and a 7-DoF robot arm avoidance task.

</details>


### [181] [Mixed-Agent Museum Tour Guide Design Improves Gendered Learning Outcomes and Visitor Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14468)
*Annette M. Masterson,Wonse Jo,Helena C. Sieh,Lionel P. Robert,,Dawn Tilbury*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 一项针对博物馆导览系统的研究发现：结合物理机器人与虚拟代理的混合系统能显著提升女性游客的学习效果，同时所有游客均更偏好该系统，因其增强了互动体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人技术日益融入博物馆等公共文化空间，为提升游客体验质量与参与度，研究旨在开发一种创新导览系统，兼顾娱乐性与教育性。

Method: 采用被试内实验设计，招募30名参与者测试不同对话风格及代理配置的混合导览系统，通过问卷、行为传感器和深度访谈多维收集数据。

Result: 体验质量与参与度在各实验条件下无显著差异；但学习效果呈现显著性别交互效应——混合系统显著提升女性参与者成绩；所有参与者均明确偏好混合团队，强调互动为核心价值。

Conclusion: 混合代理导览系统的双人对话模式存在性别差异化学习效果，但其增强的互动特性获得跨性别群体认可，为智能博物馆导览设计提供关键启示。

Abstract: Robots are increasingly integrated into everyday contexts, including museums, where they can both entertain and educate visitors. To enhance visitor experience and engagement, we present a novel mixed-agent tour guide system that combines a physical robot with a projected virtual agent that actively participates in the tour through conversation and interaction, achieving the interaction richness of two mobile agents from a single platform. We validate the system through a within-subjects study with 30 participants to assess engagement, quality of experience, and learning performance. Participants experienced different conversational styles and agent configurations, and data were collected via surveys, behavioral sensors, and interviews. Results showed that engagement and quality of experience remained consistent across conditions. Learning performance revealed a significant gender-moderated difference: the mixed-agent conditions improved learning performance for female participants. This suggests that the proposed dyadic conversational style in this paper influenced learning performance differently by gender. Nonetheless, in interviews, participants reported a greater preference for mixed-agent teams regardless of gender, citing interaction as a key factor in their experience.

</details>


### [182] [MIDAS Hand: Modular low-Impedance Direct-drive Anthropomorphic Sensing Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14487)
*Alvin Zhu,Mingzhang Zhu,Beom Jun Kim,Quanyou Wang,Jose Victor S. H. Ramos,Dennis Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MIDAS Hand是一款低成本、开源、具有触觉传感功能的人体尺寸灵巧手，通过平衡性能与可及性，为操作研究提供了一个可复现平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作受限于算法和缺乏可获取的手部硬件，现有灵巧手往往在人体尺寸形态、易制造或维护性、触觉传感和实际成本之间难以平衡。

Method: MIDAS Hand采用3D打印组件，集成16个自由度（13个主动自由度）、低反向驱动扭矩的直接驱动执行器、283个三轴触觉传感器，重量700克，物料成本低于3000美元，组装时间不足3小时，并附带完整设计文件、构建文档、控制与触觉Python API、仿真模型及遥操作管线。

Result: 通过工作空间与抓握分类分析、负载与可靠性测试、可反向驱动性测量及触觉遥操作演示，验证了MIDAS Hand提供了平衡且可复现的触觉灵巧操作平台。

Conclusion: 该研究为触觉灵巧操作和人类-机器人数据采集提供了一个均衡、可复现的硬件平台。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation is limited not only by algorithms but by a shortage of accessible hand hardware that combines human-scale morphology, ease of manufacturing or maintenance, tactile sensing, and practical cost. Existing dexterous hands tend to optimize some of these properties at the expense of others. We present MIDAS Hand, a low-cost, open-source, human-scale dexterous hand with integrated tactile sensing for manipulation research. MIDAS Hand provides 16 total degrees of freedom (DoF) with 13 active DoF, directly driven actuation with measurably low backdrive torque, and 283 three-axis tactile taxels in a compact 700 g package with a bill of materials under 3,000 USD. Built from 3D-printed components, it assembles in under three hours while providing the strength, repeatability, and maintainability needed for repeated real-world experiments. Alongside the hardware, we release a full stack: design files, build documentation, control and tactile Python APIs, simulation models, and retargeting and teleoperation pipelines. We characterize MIDAS Hand through workspace and grasp-taxonomy analysis, payload and reliability tests, backdrivability measurements, and teleoperation demonstrations with tactile sensing, showing that it offers a balanced, reproducible platform for tactile dexterous manipulation and human-to-robot data collection. Project page: https://midas-hand.com

</details>


### [183] [Safe Execution of RL Policies Via Acceleration-Based CBF-QP Constraint Enforcement for Real-World Robotic Deployments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14488)
*Bastien Muraccioli,Alice Cariou,Pierre-Alexandre Leziart,Mathieu Celerier,Arnaud Demont,Gentiane Venture,Mehdi Benallegue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has demonstrated remarkable capabilities for solving complex robotic control problems, but its lack of safety guarantees severely limits deployment on hardware. In particular, as legged robots and manipulators often operate near safety-critical boundaries, out-of-distribution states can lead to failure upon deployment. To address this, we introduce Acc-CBF-QP, an acceleration-based Quadratic Program (QP) safety filter using Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) that constrains any RL policy onto a safe set at runtime without modifying training. The method applies to unconstrained and Safe-RL policies, and enforces joint position, velocity, torque, and collision constraints within a unified optimization framework. A key contribution is the formulation of RL+QP tasks that regulate deviation from the RL command when constraints would otherwise be violated. We introduce a TorqueTask, minimizing torque deviation, and a Forward Dynamics Task, minimizing induced acceleration deviation, thus providing principled control over safety-performance trade-offs. Experiments on a 7-DoF Kinova Gen3 manipulator and a 19-DoF Unitree H1 humanoid, both in simulation and on hardware, highlight substantial reductions in constraint violations. On the real H1 hardware, a Safe-RL policy alone yielded 10.04 violations/s, which were reduced by 92% to 0.80 violations/s when augmented with Acc-CBF-QP. On the Kinova Gen3, Acc-CBF-QP fully eliminated violations. Nominal task performance of the RL objective is preserved in violation-free regimes. Under aggressive velocity commands on H1, Acc-CBF-QP improves execution by preventing constraint-induced shutdowns, yielding longer survival times. The full pipeline is open-source.

</details>


### [184] [DRIFT: Drift and Aggregation for Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14507)
*Yining Xing,Zhiyuan Liu,Zehong Ke,Wenhao Yu,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRIFT是一种高效的多假设轨迹规划器，通过单步潜在空间提案生成和直接聚合，在实时约束下产生高质量可执行轨迹，显著缩短推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端轨迹规划器需表示多种可行驾驶行为，但proposal-based方法在将提案集转化为单一计划时面临关键设计挑战；DRIFT旨在解决此问题，以提升实时性和规划质量。

Method: DRIFT采用固定深度架构，结合紧凑轨迹潜在空间的单步漂移与场景感知提案聚合。基于预训练视觉编码器特征，解码器批量生成48个提案特征（α=0.5时32个，α=0.9时16个）；轻量级聚合头整合场景、导航和自状态信息，直接预测最终轨迹，无需轨迹级质量标签；训练使用专家轨迹模仿和地图派生边界正则化器（惩罚非可行驶区域点）。

Result: 在NAVSIM navtest上，DRIFT实现89.6 PDMS和90.4 EPDMS，具备优异的可行驶区域合规性和自进度；提案生成与聚合模块在NVIDIA RTX 4090上耗时10.82 ms，全模型推理（含视觉主干）仅需66.43 ms。

Conclusion: 单步潜在提案生成和直接聚合设计为多假设运动规划提供了高效方案，平衡了提案多样性和实时执行需求。

Abstract: End-to-end trajectory planners need to represent multiple plausible driving behaviors while producing a single executable trajectory under real-time constraints. Proposal-based approaches address this ambiguity by generating multiple candidates, but converting the proposal set into a final plan remains a key design problem. We present DRIFT, a fixed-depth planner that combines one-step drifting in a compact trajectory latent space with scene-aware proposal aggregation. Conditioned on features from a pretrained visual encoder, the DRIFT Decoder generates 48 proposal features in a single batched pass, with 32 samples at alpha=0.5 and 16 samples at alpha=0.9. A lightweight Aggregation Head integrates these features with scene, navigation, and ego-state information and directly predicts the final trajectory without requiring trajectory-level quality labels for aggregation. Its output is trained with expert-trajectory imitation and a map-derived boundary regularizer that penalizes waypoints outside the drivable polygon and inside waypoints near its boundary. On NAVSIM navtest, DRIFT achieves 89.6 PDMS and 90.4 EPDMS, with strong drivable-area compliance and ego progress among the methods compared. The proposal-generation and aggregation module runs in 10.82 ms on an NVIDIA RTX 4090, while full-model inference including the visual backbone takes 66.43 ms. These results show that one-step latent proposal generation and direct aggregation provide an efficient design for multi-hypothesis motion planning.

</details>


### [185] [Communication-Efficient Relative Pose Estimation with Vision Foundation Models for Ephemeral Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14539)
*Qihang Li,Jo-Hao Huang,Jiewen Liu,Suyoung Kang,Hao Zhang,Peng Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CERPE是一种通信高效的相对姿态估计框架，针对多机器人短暂协作场景，通过固定大小描述符减少数据传输并维持非重叠情况下的相对姿态估计，实验验证其显著提升6自由度估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
